Who are all the Presidents of
France?
 
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX onto: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX res: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX prop: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
WHERE 
{
  {
    ?uri rdf:type onto:President .
    ?uri onto:country res:France .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uri rdf:type onto:Person .
    ?uri prop:title res:President_of_France .
  }
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?result a dbo:Person .
  {
    ?result dbp:office dbr:President_of_France .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?result dbp:office ?role .
    ?role a dbo:Office ;
          rdfs:label ?label .
    FILTER(langMatches(lang(?label),"en") && regex(?label, "President of France", "i"))
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?result dbp:office ?role2 .
    ?role2 rdfs:label ?labelEs .
    FILTER(langMatches(lang(?labelEs),"es") && regex(?labelEs, "Presidente de Francia", "i"))
  }
}

db26:2-en 
Which actor appeared in the most
films in a single decade?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?actor
WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?actor ((FLOOR(?year/10)*10) AS ?decade) ?film
    WHERE {
      ?film a dbo:Film ;
            dbo:starring ?actor ;
            dbo:releaseDate ?date .
      BIND (xsd:integer(SUBSTR(STR(?date),1,4)) AS ?year)
    }
  }
}
GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?film))
LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mithun_Chakraborty": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT DISTINCT (?actor AS ?result) WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?actor (MAX(?decadeCount) AS ?maxCnt) WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?actor ?decadeStart (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?decadeCount) WHERE {
          ?film rdf:type dbo:Film ;
                dbo:starring ?actor .
          OPTIONAL { ?film dbo:releaseDate ?date . }
          OPTIONAL { ?film dbp:released ?released . }
          BIND(COALESCE(?date, ?released) AS ?reldate)
          FILTER(BOUND(?reldate))
          BIND(xsd:integer(SUBSTR(str(?reldate), 1, 4)) AS ?yr)
          BIND((?yr - (?yr % 10)) AS ?decadeStart)
        } GROUP BY ?actor ?decadeStart
      }
    } GROUP BY ?actor
  }
  {
    SELECT (MAX(?actorMax) AS ?globalMax) WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?actor_ (MAX(?decadeCount_) AS ?actorMax) WHERE {
          {
            SELECT ?actor_ ?decadeStart_ (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film_) AS ?decadeCount_) WHERE {
              ?film_ rdf:type dbo:Film ;
                     dbo:starring ?actor_ .
              OPTIONAL { ?film_ dbo:releaseDate ?date_ . }
              OPTIONAL { ?film_ dbp:released ?released_ . }
              BIND(COALESCE(?date_, ?released_) AS ?reldate_)
              FILTER(BOUND(?reldate_))
              BIND(xsd:integer(SUBSTR(str(?reldate_), 1, 4)) AS ?yr_)
              BIND((?yr_ - (?yr_ % 10)) AS ?decadeStart_)
            } GROUP BY ?actor_ ?decadeStart_
          }
        } GROUP BY ?actor_
      }
    }
  }
  FILTER(?maxCnt = ?globalMax)
}
 
{}

db26:2-es 
¿Qué actor apareció en la mayor
cantidad de películas en una sola
década?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT ?actor
WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?actor ((FLOOR(?year/10)*10) AS ?decade) ?film
    WHERE {
      ?film a dbo:Film ;
            dbo:starring ?actor ;
            dbo:releaseDate ?date .
      BIND (xsd:integer(SUBSTR(STR(?date),1,4)) AS ?year)
    }
  }
}
GROUP BY ?actor ?decade
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?film))
LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mithun_Chakraborty": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?actor ?decadeStart (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?actor rdf:type dbo:Actor .
      ?film rdf:type dbo:Film .
      ?film dbo:starring ?actor .
      ?film dbo:releaseDate ?date .
      BIND(YEAR(?date) AS ?yr) .
      BIND((?yr - MOD(?yr, 10)) AS ?decadeStart) .
    }
    GROUP BY ?actor ?decadeStart
  }
  {
    SELECT (MAX(?c) AS ?maxCount) WHERE {
      SELECT ?a ?ds (COUNT(DISTINCT ?f) AS ?c) WHERE {
        ?a rdf:type dbo:Actor .
        ?f rdf:type dbo:Film .
        ?f dbo:starring ?a .
        ?f dbo:releaseDate ?d .
        BIND(YEAR(?d) AS ?y) .
        BIND((?y - MOD(?y, 10)) AS ?ds) .
      }
      GROUP BY ?a ?ds
    }
  }
  FILTER(?count = ?maxCount) .
  BIND(?actor AS ?result) .
}
 
{}

db26:3-en 
Who (either an actor or a musician)
has received the highest number of
awards?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?person
WHERE {
  {
    ?person a dbo:Actor .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?person a dbo:MusicalArtist .
  }

  ?person dbo:award ?award .
}
GROUP BY ?person
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?award))
LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Federico_Luppi": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?person ?awardCount WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?person (COUNT(DISTINCT ?a) AS ?awardCount) WHERE {
      {
        { ?person a ?type . FILTER(?type IN (dbo:Actor, dbo:MusicalArtist, dbo:Musician)) }
        UNION
        { ?person dbo:occupation ?typeOcc . FILTER(?typeOcc IN (dbo:Actor, dbo:MusicalArtist, dbo:Musician)) . BIND(?typeOcc AS ?type) }
      }
      OPTIONAL {
        { ?person dbo:award ?a } 
        UNION { ?person dbp:awards ?a } 
        UNION { ?person dbp:award ?a }
        UNION { ?person dbo:awards ?a }
      }
    } GROUP BY ?person
  }
  {
    SELECT (MAX(?awardCount) AS ?maxCount) WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?p (COUNT(DISTINCT ?a2) AS ?awardCount) WHERE {
          {
            { ?p a ?type2 . FILTER(?type2 IN (dbo:Actor, dbo:MusicalArtist, dbo:Musician)) }
            UNION
            { ?p dbo:occupation ?type2Occ . FILTER(?type2Occ IN (dbo:Actor, dbo:MusicalArtist, dbo:Musician)) . BIND(?type2Occ AS ?type2) }
          }
          OPTIONAL {
            { ?p dbo:award ?a2 } 
            UNION { ?p dbp:awards ?a2 } 
            UNION { ?p dbp:award ?a2 }
            UNION { ?p dbo:awards ?a2 }
          }
        } GROUP BY ?p
      }
    }
  }
  FILTER(?awardCount = ?maxCount)
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?result (COUNT(DISTINCT ?award1) AS ?awardCount) WHERE {
      { ?result a dbo:Actor } UNION { ?result a dbo:MusicalArtist }
      ?result dbo:award ?award1 .
    } GROUP BY ?result
  }
  {
    SELECT (MAX(?c) AS ?maxCount) WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?p (COUNT(DISTINCT ?awardItem2) AS ?c) WHERE {
          { ?p a dbo:Actor } UNION { ?p a dbo:MusicalArtist }
          ?p dbo:award ?awardItem2 .
        } GROUP BY ?p
      }
    }
  }
  FILTER(?awardCount = ?maxCount)
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>

WHERE {
  {
    ?artist a dbo:MusicalArtist .
  }
  UNION
  {

  }

  ?album dbo:artist ?artist .
}
GROUP BY ?artist
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?album))
LIMIT 5

SELECT DISTINCT ?result ?count WHERE {
  {
    SELECT (?artist AS ?result) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?album) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?album a dbo:Album .
      {
        ?album dbo:artist ?artist .

        ?album dbp:artist ?artist .

      ?artist a ?artistType .
      FILTER(isURI(?artist) && ?artistType IN (dbo:MusicalArtist, dbo:Band, dbo:Artist, dbo:ClassicalMusicArtist))
    }
    GROUP BY ?artist
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 5
  }
}

db26:5-en 
Which are the top 3 taxonomic
kingdoms that have the largest
number of endangered species?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT ?kingdom
WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?species ?kingdom
    WHERE {
      ?species a dbo:Species ;
              dbo:conservationStatus ?status ;
              dbo:kingdom ?kingdom .
      FILTER (regex(str(?status), "Endangered|Critically endangered", "i"))
    }
  }
}
GROUP BY ?kingdom
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?species))
LIMIT 3
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,
    "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Plantae": 1
}

PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?kingdom (COUNT(DISTINCT ?species) AS ?count) WHERE {
  ?species a dbo:Species ;
           dbo:conservationStatus ?status ;
           dbp:kingdom ?kingdom .
  FILTER(?status = dbr:Endangered)
}
GROUP BY ?kingdom
ORDER BY DESC(?count)
LIMIT 3

db26:5-es 
¿Cuáles son los 3 reinos
taxonómicos con el mayor número de
especies en peligro de extinción?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT ?kingdom
WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?species ?kingdom
    WHERE {
      ?species a dbo:Species ;
              dbo:conservationStatus ?status ;
              dbo:kingdom ?kingdom .
      FILTER (regex(str(?status), "Endangered|Critically endangered", "i"))
    }
  }
}
GROUP BY ?kingdom
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?species))
LIMIT 3
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Animal": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Plant": 1,
    "http://es.dbpedia.org/resource/Plantae": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?kingdom (COUNT(DISTINCT ?species) AS ?count) WHERE {
  { { ?species dbo:kingdom ?kingdom } UNION { ?species dbp:kingdom ?kingdom } } .
  ?species rdf:type dbo:Species .
  { { ?species dbo:conservationStatus ?status } UNION { ?species dbp:status ?status } } .
  OPTIONAL { ?status rdfs:label ?statusLabel . }
  FILTER(
    regex(str(?status),"endangered","i")
    || (bound(?statusLabel) && regex(str(?statusLabel),"endangered","i"))
    || regex(str(?status),"en peligro","i")
    || (bound(?statusLabel) && regex(str(?statusLabel),"en peligro","i"))
    || regex(str(?status),"peligro de extinción","i")
    || (bound(?statusLabel) && regex(str(?statusLabel),"peligro de extinción","i"))
    || regex(str(?status),"\\bEN\\b","i")
    || (bound(?statusLabel) && regex(str(?statusLabel),"\\bEN\\b","i"))
    || ?status = dbr:Endangered
  )
}
GROUP BY ?kingdom
ORDER BY DESC(?count)
LIMIT 3

  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blender_(software)> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/license> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/GNU_General_Public_License>
}

    "true": 1

PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

  { dbr:Blender_(software) dbo:license ?lic .
    FILTER(regex(str(?lic), "GNU", "i"))
  }
  UNION
  { dbr:Blender_(software) dbp:license ?lic2 .
    FILTER(regex(str(?lic2), "GNU", "i"))
  }
  UNION
  { dbr:Blender_(software) dbo:license ?L .
    ?L rdfs:label ?label .
    FILTER(langMatches(lang(?label),"en") && regex(?label, "GNU", "i"))
  }
  UNION
  { dbr:Blender_(software) dbp:license ?L2 .
    ?L2 rdfs:label ?label2 .
    FILTER(langMatches(lang(?label2),"en") && regex(?label2, "GNU", "i"))
  }

db26:11-es 
¿Quiénes son los primeros ministros
del Reino Unido?
 
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX yago: <http://dbpedia.org/class/yago/>
PREFIX onto: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX res: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX prop: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
WHERE
{
  {
    ?uri rdf:type yago:PrimeMinistersOfTheUnitedKingdom .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uri rdf:type onto:PrimeMinister .
    ?uri prop:title res:Prime_Minister_of_the_United_Kingdom .
  }
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?result a dbo:Person .
  {
    # direct link to the Prime Minister office resource (use dbp:office)
    ?result dbp:office dbr:Prime_Minister_of_the_United_Kingdom .
  }
  UNION
  {
    # office with a label containing "Prime Minister"
    ?result dbp:office ?office .
    ?office rdfs:label ?officeLabel .
    FILTER(langMatches(lang(?officeLabel),"EN"))
    FILTER(regex(?officeLabel, "Prime Minister", "i"))
  }
  UNION
  {
    # property in dbp that mentions Prime Minister in its string value
    ?result dbp:office ?officeStr .
    FILTER(regex(str(?officeStr), "Prime Minister", "i"))
  }
}

db26:12-en 
What are the 10 oldest works by
Mark Twain?
 
SELECT ?uri WHERE {
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/property/author> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mark_Twain> ;
      <http://dbpedia.org/property/releaseDate> ?date .
}
ORDER BY ASC(?date)
LIMIT 10
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Adventures_of_Huckleberry_Finn": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Innocents_Abroad": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mark_Twain's_(Burlesque)_Autobiography_and_First_Romance": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Roughing_It": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_Gilded_Age:_A_Tale_of_Today": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/A_Tramp_Abroad": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Life_on_the_Mississippi": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Merry_Tales": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/The_American_Claimant": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT ?result (MIN(?date) AS ?minDate) WHERE {
  ?result (dbo:author|dbp:author|foaf:maker) dbr:Mark_Twain .
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbo:publicationDate ?date . }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbo:releaseDate ?date . }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:year ?date . }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:published ?date . }
}
GROUP BY ?result
HAVING(BOUND(?minDate))
ORDER BY ASC(?minDate)
LIMIT 10
 
{}

}
ORDER BY ASC(?date)
LIMIT 10

    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Roughing_It": 1,

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?result dbp:author dbr:Mark_Twain .
  
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbo:publicationDate ?pd1. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:publicationDate ?pd2. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:releaseDate ?pd3. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:published ?pd4. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:pubDate ?pd5. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbo:date ?pd6. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:date ?pd7. }
  OPTIONAL { ?result dbp:year ?pd8. }
  
  BIND(COALESCE(?pd1, ?pd2, ?pd3, ?pd4, ?pd5, ?pd6, ?pd7, ?pd8) AS ?date)
  FILTER(bound(?date))
}
ORDER BY ASC(?date)
LIMIT 10

db26:13-en 
In which settlement was the wife of
Barack Obama born?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX res: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri
WHERE {
  res:Barack_Obama dbo:spouse ?x .
  ?x dbo:birthPlace ?uri .
  ?uri rdf:type dbo:Settlement .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Calumet_Park,_Illinois": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  dbr:Barack_Obama dbp:spouse ?wife .
  {
    ?wife dbp:birthPlace ?result .
  } UNION {
    ?wife dbp:birthPlace ?place .
    ?place dbo:settlement ?result .
  }
}
 
{
    "DeYoung, Illinois, U.S.": 1
}

¿Dónde nació la jugadora de
bádminton que ganó la medalla de
plata en la prueba de dobles
femeninos de los Juegos Olímpicos
de Verano de 2012?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Badminton_at_the_2012_Summer_Olympics_–_Women's_doubles> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/silverMedalist> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/placeOfBirth> ?uri .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Kumamoto_Prefecture": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Kami-Amakusa": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Japan": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?player a dbo:BadmintonPlayer .
  ?player dbo:birthPlace ?result .
  
  dbr:Badminton_at_the_2012_Summer_Olympics_%E2%80%93_Women%27s_doubles dbp:silver ?team .
  ?team dbp:players ?player .
}
 
{}

    "set_P": 0.0,
    "set_recall": 0.0,
    "set_F": 0.0
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  { ?result dbo:creator dbr:Stan_Lee . }
  UNION
  { ?result dbp:creator ?creator .
    FILTER(regex(str(?creator), "Stan Lee", "i"))
  }
  FILTER(?result != dbr:Stan_Lee)
}

}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/United_States": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Kahiltna_Peaks": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Peters_Dome": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mount_Foraker": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Kahiltna_Dome": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mount_Brooks_(Alaska)": 1

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?child ?country WHERE {
  {
    { ?child dbo:parentPeak dbr:Denali. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbp:parentPeak dbr:Denali. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbp:parentPeak dbr:Denali. }
    UNION
    { dbr:Denali dbo:subsidiaryPeak ?child. }
    UNION
    { dbr:Denali dbp:subsidiaryPeak ?child. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbo:partOf dbr:Denali. }
  }
  {
    { ?child dbo:locatedInArea ?loc . ?loc dbo:country ?country. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbp:country ?country. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbo:location ?loc . ?loc dbo:country ?country. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbp:location ?loc . ?loc dbo:country ?country. }
    UNION
    { ?child dbp:location ?country. }
  }
}

db26:18-es 
¿En qué países se encuentran los
picos secundarios del Denali y
cuáles son?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri ?x WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/parentPeak> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Denali> .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/location> ?y .
  ?y <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/country> ?uri .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/United_States": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Kahiltna_Peaks": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Peters_Dome": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mount_Foraker": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Kahiltna_Dome": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Mount_Brooks_(Alaska)": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    ?peak dbp:parent_peak dbr:Denali .
  } UNION {
    ?peak dbo:parentMountain dbr:Denali .
  }

  OPTIONAL { ?peak dbo:country ?country. }
  OPTIONAL {
    ?peak dbo:location ?loc .
    ?loc dbo:country ?country .
  }

  OPTIONAL { ?peak rdfs:label ?pl_es FILTER(langMatches(lang(?pl_es),"es")) }
  OPTIONAL { ?peak rdfs:label ?pl_en FILTER(langMatches(lang(?pl_en),"en")) }
  OPTIONAL { ?country rdfs:label ?cl_es FILTER(langMatches(lang(?cl_es),"es")) }
  OPTIONAL { ?country rdfs:label ?cl_en FILTER(langMatches(lang(?cl_en),"en")) }

  BIND(COALESCE(?pl_es, ?pl_en, STRAFTER(STR(?peak), "resource/")) AS ?peakLabel)
  BIND(COALESCE(?cl_es, ?cl_en, STR(?country), "Unknown") AS ?countryLabel)

  BIND(CONCAT(?peakLabel, " - ", ?countryLabel) AS ?result)
}
 
{}
 
{
    "set_P": 0.0,
    "set_recall": 0.0,
    "set_F": 0.0
}

  dbr:Pep_Guardiola dbo:coachedTeam ?team .

PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

  { ?result dbo:team ?team } UNION { ?result dbp:team ?team } .
  VALUES ?team { dbr:FC_Barcelona dbr:FC_Bayern_Munich dbr:Manchester_City_F.C. } .
  FILTER(?result != dbr:Pep_Guardiola)
}

db26:20-en 
Through which cities do the rivers
originating in Yellowstone National
Park flow?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/sourcePlace> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Yellowstone_National_Park> .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/city> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/River> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Lewiston,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tri-Cities,_Washington": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Homedale,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Blackfoot,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Twin_Falls,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Burley,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/American_Falls,_Idaho": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Idaho_Falls,_Idaho": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    ?river dbo:sourceLocation dbr:Yellowstone_National_Park .
  } UNION {
    ?river dbo:source dbr:Yellowstone_National_Park .
  } UNION {
    ?river dbp:source dbr:Yellowstone_National_Park .
  } UNION {
    ?river dbo:origin dbr:Yellowstone_National_Park .
  }
  {
    ?result a dbo:PopulatedPlace .
    {
      {
        ?result dbo:river ?river .
      } UNION {
        ?result dbp:river ?river .
      } UNION {
        ?result dbo:waterway ?river .
      } UNION {
        ?result dbp:waterway ?river .
      } UNION {
        ?river dbp:cities ?result .
      } UNION {
        ?river dbp:towns ?result .
      } UNION {
        ?river dbp:city ?result .
      }
    }
  }
}

db26:21-en 
Which alliance has the most member
airlines?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
SELECT ?uri
WHERE {
  ?airline a dbo:Airline .
  ?airline dbp:alliance ?uri .
}
GROUP BY ?uri
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline))
OFFSET 0 LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Star_Alliance": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?alliance (COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline) AS ?members) WHERE {
      ?airline dbo:alliance ?alliance .
      ?airline rdf:type dbo:Airline .
    }
    GROUP BY ?alliance
    ORDER BY DESC(?members)
    LIMIT 1
  }
  BIND(?alliance AS ?result)
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Star_Alliance": 1
}

    "set_P": 1.0,
    "set_recall": 1.0,
    "set_F": 1.0

db26:21-es 
¿Qué alianza tiene la mayor
cantidad de aerolíneas miembros?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
SELECT ?uri
WHERE {
  ?airline a dbo:Airline .
  ?airline dbp:alliance ?uri .
}
GROUP BY ?uri
ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline))
OFFSET 0 LIMIT 1
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Star_Alliance": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    SELECT ?alliance (COUNT(DISTINCT ?airline) AS ?count) WHERE {
      ?airline dbp:alliances ?alliance .
    }
    GROUP BY ?alliance
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 1
  }
  BIND(?alliance AS ?result)
}

  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/tenant> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/BC_Lions> .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/builder> ?uri .
  ?x a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Stadium> .
  ?uri a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Company> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Nussli_Group": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/PCL_Construction": 1

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

  ?stadium dbp:tenants dbr:BC_Lions .

    ?stadium dbp:builder ?result .

  UNION { ?stadium dbo:builder ?result . }
  UNION { ?stadium dbp:builtBy ?result . }
  UNION { ?stadium dbo:architect ?result . }
  UNION { ?stadium dbp:contractor ?result . }
}

  ?x a <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Stadium> .

    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Nussli_Group": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/PCL_Construction": 1

PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?stadium ?tenantProp dbr:BC_Lions .
  FILTER(?tenantProp IN (dbo:tenant, dbp:tenant, dbo:tenants, dbp:tenants))

    ?stadium ?builderProp ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?result ?builderProp ?stadium .
  }
  FILTER(?builderProp IN (dbp:builder, dbp:built, dbp:builtBy, dbo:builder, dbo:built, dbp:contractor, dbo:contractor, dbp:architect, dbo:architect))
}

¿Cuál era el partido del predecesor
de Yingluck Shinawatra?

WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Yingluck_Shinawatra> <http://dbpedia.org/property/predecessor> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/party> ?uri .
}

    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Democrat_Party_(Thailand)": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  dbr:Yingluck_Shinawatra dbo:predecessor ?pred .
  ?pred dbo:party ?party .
  OPTIONAL { ?party rdfs:label ?labelEs FILTER(lang(?labelEs) = "es") }
  OPTIONAL { ?party rdfs:label ?labelEn FILTER(langMatches(lang(?labelEn),"en")) }
  BIND(COALESCE(?labelEs, ?labelEn, STR(?party)) AS ?result)
}

    "Democrat Party (Thailand)": 1

  OPTIONAL { ?result rdf:type ?type . }
}

db26:28-en 
Name some television shows related
to the one whose opening theme was
composed by Bear McCreary.
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/themeMusicComposer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/related> ?x .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/TelevisionShow> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fear_the_Walking_Dead": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Talking_Dead": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  # find the show whose opening theme was composed by Bear McCreary
  {
    ?show dbp:themeMusicComposer dbr:Bear_McCreary .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?show dbo:themeMusicComposer dbr:Bear_McCreary .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?show dbo:openingTheme ?theme .
    ?theme dbo:composer dbr:Bear_McCreary .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?show dbp:openingTheme ?theme .
    ?theme dbp:composer dbr:Bear_McCreary .
  }
  
  # get shows related to that show (try common related properties)
  {
    ?show dbo:related ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?show dbp:related ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?show dbo:relatedFunctions ?result .
  }
  
  # restrict results to television series
  ?result a dbo:TelevisionSeries .
  
  FILTER(?result != ?show)
}
 
{}
 
{
    "set_P": 0.0,
    "set_recall": 0.0,
    "set_F": 0.0
}
db26:28-es 
Nombra algunos programas de
televisión relacionados con aquel
cuya música de apertura fue
compuesta por Bear McCreary.
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/themeMusicComposer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Bear_McCreary> .
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/related> ?x .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/TelevisionShow> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Fear_the_Walking_Dead": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Talking_Dead": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  # aceptar tanto la propiedad como el literal que contenga el nombre
  ?show ?composerProp ?composer .
  VALUES ?composerProp { dbo:composer dbo:themeMusicComposer dbp:themeMusicComposer dbp:composer dbo:associatedMusicalArtist }
  FILTER( ?composer = dbr:Bear_McCreary || STR(?composer) = "Bear McCreary" )

  # buscar programas relacionados al show cuya música de apertura compuso Bear McCreary
  ?show (dbo:wikiPageWikiLink|dbo:relatedWork) ?result .
  ?result rdf:type dbo:TelevisionSeries .
}
 
{}

    "set_P": 0.0,
    "set_recall": 0.0,
    "set_F": 0.0

db26:29-en 
Where was the song 'Imagine'
recorded?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Imagine_(John_Lennon_song)> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/recordedIn> ?uri
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tittenhurst_Park": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Record_Plant": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/New_York": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

How many rivers do structures
designed by Gustave Eiffel cross?

WHERE
{
  ?structure <http://dbpedia.org/property/designer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Gustave_Eiffel> .
  ?structure <http://dbpedia.org/property/crosses> ?river .
}

    "1": 1
}

PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>

SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?river) AS ?count) WHERE {
  {
    ?structure dbo:designer dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbp:designer dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbo:architect dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbp:architect dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  }
  ?structure dbo:crosses ?river .
  ?river rdf:type dbo:River .
}

estructuras diseñadas por Gustave
Eiffel?

WHERE
{
  ?structure <http://dbpedia.org/property/designer> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Gustave_Eiffel> .

}

PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?river) AS ?result) WHERE {
  {
    ?structure dbo:designer dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbo:engineer dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbo:architect dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbp:designer dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbp:engineer dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  } UNION {
    ?structure dbp:architect dbr:Gustave_Eiffel .
  }
  VALUES ?crossProp { dbo:crosses dbp:crosses dbp:crossing }
  ?river ?crossProp ?structure .

    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Pop_music": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Electronic_dance_music": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Soul_music": 1,

  OPTIONAL { ?origin rdfs:label ?labelEn FILTER(lang(?labelEn) = "en") }

db26:36-es 
¿Cuál es la superficie terrestre
del país donde se encuentra la
Universidad de Oxford?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
SELECT ?area WHERE {
  dbr:University_of_Oxford dbo:country ?c .
  ?c dbp:areaKm ?area .
}
 
{
    "130279": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  { dbr:University_of_Oxford dbo:country ?country. }
  UNION
  { dbr:University_of_Oxford dbp:country ?country. }
  
  OPTIONAL { ?country dbo:areaLand ?areaLand. }
  OPTIONAL { ?country dbo:areaTotal ?areaTotal. }
  OPTIONAL { ?country dbp:areaKm2 ?areaKm2. }
  OPTIONAL { ?country dbp:area ?area. }
  
  BIND(COALESCE(?areaLand, ?areaTotal, ?areaKm2, ?area) AS ?result)
  FILTER(BOUND(?result))
}

served?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Stewart_Bovell> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/militaryBranch> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/motto> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/MilitaryUnit> .
}
 
{
    "\"Through Adversity to the Stars\"": 1,
    "Per Ardua ad Astra": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  dbr:Stewart_Bovell dbo:militaryUnit ?unit .
  {
    ?unit dbo:motto ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?unit dbp:motto ?result .
  }
}
 
{}
 
{
    "set_P": 0.0,
    "set_recall": 0.0,
    "set_F": 0.0
}

¿Cuál es el lema de la unidad
militar en la que sirvió Stewart
Bovell?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Stewart_Bovell> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/militaryBranch> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/motto> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/MilitaryUnit> .
}
 
{
    "\"Through Adversity to the Stars\"": 1,
    "Per Ardua ad Astra": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  dbr:Stewart_Bovell dbp:unit ?unit .
  {
    ?unit dbo:motto ?result
  } UNION {
    ?unit dbp:motto ?result
  } UNION {
    ?unit dbp:lema ?result
  }
}

db26:38-en 
Which person has been married to
both Jennifer Aniston and Angelina
Jolie?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Jennifer_Aniston> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/spouse> ?uri .
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Angelina_Jolie> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/spouse> ?uri .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Brad_Pitt": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    { dbr:Jennifer_Aniston dbo:spouse ?result }
    UNION { ?result dbo:spouse dbr:Jennifer_Aniston }
    UNION { dbr:Jennifer_Aniston dbp:spouse ?result }
    UNION { ?result dbp:spouse dbr:Jennifer_Aniston }
  }
  {
    { dbr:Angelina_Jolie dbo:spouse ?result }
    UNION { ?result dbo:spouse dbr:Angelina_Jolie }
    UNION { dbr:Angelina_Jolie dbp:spouse ?result }
    UNION { ?result dbp:spouse dbr:Angelina_Jolie }
  }
  OPTIONAL { ?result a dbo:Person }
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Brad_Pitt": 1
}

    "set_P": 1.0,
    "set_recall": 1.0,
    "set_F": 1.0

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?result rdf:type ?type .
  FILTER(?type IN (dbo:Software, dbo:ComputerProgram))
  
  FILTER EXISTS {
    ?result ?p1 ?os1 .
    FILTER(?p1 IN (dbo:operatingSystem, dbo:platform, dbp:operatingSystem, dbp:platform, dbp:operating_system))
    FILTER(
      ?os1 = dbr:Linux ||
      CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?os1)), "linux")
    )
  }
  FILTER EXISTS {
    ?result ?p2 ?os2 .
    FILTER(?p2 IN (dbo:operatingSystem, dbo:platform, dbp:operatingSystem, dbp:platform, dbp:operating_system))
    FILTER(
      ?os2 = dbr:MacOS ||
      ?os2 = dbr:Mac_OS_X ||
      CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?os2)), "mac") ||
      CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?os2)), "os x") ||
      CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?os2)), "macos")
    )
  }
}
ORDER BY ?result

  {
    ?uni a dbo:University ;
         (dbo:city|dbp:city|dbo:location|dbp:location) ?city .
    ?city (dbo:region|dbp:region) dbr:Metro_Manila .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uni a dbo:University ;
         (dbo:city|dbp:city|dbo:location|dbp:location) dbr:Metro_Manila .
  }
  {
    ?uni dbp:affiliations ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uni dbp:academic_affiliations ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uni dbo:affiliation ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uni dbp:affiliation ?result .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?uni dbp:academic_affiliations ?result .
  }
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    ?person dbo:award dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
    # other awards recorded as resources for persons with the medal
    ?person dbo:award ?result .
    FILTER(?result != dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom)
  }
  UNION
  {
    # persons recorded as having the medal in dbp:awards (literal)
    ?person dbp:awards ?medraw .
    FILTER(regex(str(?medraw), "Presidential Medal of Freedom", "i"))
    # try to get other awards modelled as dbo:award for these persons
    ?person dbo:award ?result .
    FILTER(?result != dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom)
  }
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom dbp:recipients ?person .
  } UNION {
    ?person dbp:awards dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
  } UNION {
    ?person dbp:awards dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom .
  } 
  .
  {
    ?person dbp:awards ?result .
  } UNION {
    ?person dbp:awards ?result .
  }
  FILTER(?result != dbr:Presidential_Medal_of_Freedom)
}

PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  ?result rdf:type dbo:Saint .
  {
    ?result dbp:veneratedIn ?v .
    FILTER( ?v = dbr:Christianity || regex(str(?v),"Christian","i") )
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?result dbo:veneratedIn ?v .
    FILTER( ?v = dbr:Christianity || regex(str(?v),"Christian","i") )
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?result dbp:venerated_in ?v .
    FILTER( ?v = dbr:Christianity || regex(str(?v),"Christian","i") )
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?result dbp:venerated ?v .
    FILTER( ?v = dbr:Christianity || regex(str(?v),"Christian","i") )
  }
}

db26:46-en 
Which city is the radio station
located in that is a sister station
of WXME?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/property/sisterStations> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/RadioStation> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  { dbr:WXME dbp:sisterStations ?s. } UNION { ?s dbp:sisterStations dbr:WXME. }

  {
    ?s dbo:city ?result.
  } UNION {
    ?s dbo:location ?result.
  } UNION {
    ?s dbp:city ?result.
  } UNION {
    ?s dbo:city ?cityRes.
    ?cityRes rdfs:label ?result.
    FILTER(lang(?result) = "en")
  } UNION {
    ?s dbo:location ?cityRes.
    ?cityRes rdfs:label ?result.
    FILTER(lang(?result) = "en")
  } UNION {
    ?s dbp:city ?cityRes.
    ?cityRes rdfs:label ?result.
    FILTER(lang(?result) = "en")
  }
}

    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1,
    "Maine": 1,
    "Monticello, Maine": 1

db26:46-es 
¿En qué ciudad está ubicada la
emisora de radio que es una
estación hermana de WXME?
 
SELECT DISTINCT ?uri WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/WXME> <http://dbpedia.org/property/sisterStations> ?x .
  ?x <http://dbpedia.org/property/city> ?uri .
  ?x <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/RadioStation> .
}
 
{
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Maine": 1,
    "http://dbpedia.org/resource/Monticello,_Maine": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  {
    dbr:WXME_(FM) ?rel ?sister .
  } UNION {
    ?sister ?rel dbr:WXME_(FM) .
  }
  ?sister a dbo:RadioStation .
  
  OPTIONAL { ?sister dbo:city ?cityRes . }
  OPTIONAL { ?sister dbp:city ?cityLit . }
  OPTIONAL { ?sister dbo:location ?locRes . }
  OPTIONAL { ?sister dbp:location ?locLit . }
  OPTIONAL { ?sister dbp:locationCity ?locCity . }

  BIND( COALESCE(?cityRes, ?cityLit, ?locRes, ?locLit, ?locCity) AS ?result )
  FILTER(BOUND(?result))
}

between Tokyo and Delhi?

WHERE {

  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi> dbo:populationTotal ?p2 .
}

PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>

  dbr:Tokyo dbp:populationTotal ?popTokyo .

db26:49-es 
¿Cuál es la diferencia de población
entre Tokio y Delhi?
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
SELECT DISTINCT (?p2 - ?p1 AS ?result)
WHERE {
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Tokyo> dbo:populationTotal ?p1 .
  <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Delhi> dbo:populationTotal ?p2 .
}
 
{
    "3129336": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

SELECT DISTINCT ?result WHERE {
  OPTIONAL { dbr:Tokyo dbo:populationTotal ?tokyoPop1. }
  OPTIONAL { dbr:Tokyo dbo:populationTotal ?tokyoPop2. }
  OPTIONAL { dbr:New_Delhi dbo:populationTotal ?delhiPop1. }
  OPTIONAL { dbr:New_Delhi dbo:populationTotal ?delhiPop2. }

  FILTER((bound(?tokyoPop1) || bound(?tokyoPop2)) && (bound(?delhiPop1) || bound(?delhiPop2)))

  BIND(REPLACE(str(COALESCE(?tokyoPop1, ?tokyoPop2)), "[^0-9\\.\\-]", "") AS ?tokyoClean)
  BIND(REPLACE(str(COALESCE(?delhiPop1, ?delhiPop2)), "[^0-9\\.\\-]", "") AS ?delhiClean)
  BIND(xsd:decimal(?tokyoClean) AS ?tokyoPop)
  BIND(xsd:decimal(?delhiClean) AS ?delhiPop)
  BIND(ABS(?tokyoPop - ?delhiPop) AS ?result)
}
 
{
    "12935504.0": 1
}

db26:50-en 
How many movies there are in French
language?
 
SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?uri) as ?uriCount) WHERE {
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/property/language> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/French_language> .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Film>
}
 
{
    "789": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?count) WHERE {
  {
    ?film rdf:type dbo:Film ;
          dbo:language dbr:French .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?film rdf:type dbo:Film ;
          dbo:originalLanguage dbr:French .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?film rdf:type dbo:Film ;
          dbo:language ?lang .
    ?lang rdfs:label "French"@en .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?film rdf:type dbo:Film ;
          dbo:originalLanguage ?lang .
    ?lang rdfs:label "French"@en .
  }
  UNION
  {
    ?film rdf:type dbo:Film ;
          dbp:language ?langLit .
    FILTER(
      lcase(str(?langLit)) = "french" ||
      lcase(str(?langLit)) = "fr" ||
      regex(str(?langLit), "French", "i") ||
      regex(str(?langLit), "\\bfr\\b", "i")
    )
  }
}

db26:50-es 
¿Cuántas películas hay en idioma
francés?
 
SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?uri) as ?uriCount) WHERE {
  ?uri <http://dbpedia.org/property/language> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/French_language> .
  ?uri <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/Film>
}
 
{
    "789": 1
}
 
PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?film) AS ?count) WHERE {
  ?film a dbo:Film .
  {
    # language as a language resource
    ?film dbo:language dbr:French_language .
  }
  UNION
  {
    # language as a dbp property pointing to the resource
    ?film dbp:language dbr:French_language .
  }
  UNION
  {
    # language as a literal mentioning "French" (case-insensitive)
    ?film dbp:language ?langLit .
    FILTER(isLiteral(?langLit) && regex(str(?langLit), "french|français", "i"))
  }
  UNION
  {
    # language as a resource whose label indicates French
    ?film dbo:language ?langRes .
    ?langRes rdfs:label ?lbl .
    FILTER(regex(str(?lbl), "french|français", "i"))
  }
}
